<writtenStatements>

<heading>Non-Whites appointed as acting judges/judges</heading>

<question by="#swart" to="#minister_of_justice">

<num>623.</num>

<from>Mr R A F SWART</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Justice:</p>

<p>Whether any persons who are not White were appointed as (a) acting judges and (b) judges within the Republic in 1988; if not, why not; if so, (i) who were so appointed and (ii) (aa) when and (bb) where was each such person appointed?</p>

<p>B1129E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_justice" to="#swart">

<from>The MINISTER OF JUSTICE:</from>

<ol>

<li>(a) No</li>

<li>(b) No</li>

</ol>

<p>The State President may in terms of section 10(1)(a) of the Supreme Court Act, 1959 (Act 59 of 1959) appoint fit and proper persons as judges of the Republic of South Africa.</p>

<p>At present there are only two Indian advocates namely Advocates I Mahomed SC and H E Mall SC, who hold the distinction of <i>Senior Consultus.</i></p>

<ol>

<li>(i) Falls away</li>

<li>(ii)

<ol>

<li>(aa) Falls away</li>

<li>(bb) Falls away</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Security of State: sentences</heading>

<question by="#swart" to="#minister_of_justice">

<num>624.</num>

<from>Mr R A F SWART</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Justice:</p>

<p>How many (a) males and (b) females were serving sentences in 1988 for offences against the security of the State which exceeded (i) ten, (ii) five and (iii) two years?</p>

<p>B1130E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_justice" to="#swart">

<from>The MINISTER OF JUSTICE:</from>

<ol>

<li>(a)

<ol>

<li>(i) 148</li>

<li>(ii) 113</li>

<li>(iii) 80</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(b)

<ol>

<li>(i) 1</li>

<li>(ii) 6</li>

<li>(iii) 1</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Security of State: persons released</heading>

<question by="#swart" to="#minister_of_justice">

<num>625.</num>

<from>Mr R A F SWART</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Justice:</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) How many (a) males and (b) females serving sentences for offences against the security of the State were released in 1988;</li>

<li>(2) whether any of these persons were released as a result of the State President&#x2019;s offer of freedom to long-term prisoners on condition that they renounce violence; if so, (a) how many and (b) in respect of what date is this information furnished?</li>

</ol>

<p>B1131E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_justice" to="#swart">

<from>The MINISTER OF JUSTICE:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1)

<ol>

<li>(a) 42</li>

<li>(b) 3</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(2) Yes

<ol>

<li>(a) Four of the persons who were released, renounced violence. The fact that they renounced violence was an important factor which was <span class="col_1427-1428" refersTo="page_0752"/>considered together with individual circumstances and all other relevant factors which are normally taken into account when the release of prisoners is considered.</li>

<li>(b) 1 January 1988 and 31 December 1988.</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Intimidation: convictions</heading>

<question by="#suzman" to="#minister_of_justice">

<num>626.</num>

<from>Mrs H SUZMAN</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Justice:</p>

<p>Whether any persons were convicted in 1988 of offences under the Intimidation Act, No 72 of 1982; if so, how many persons in each race group?</p>

<p>B1132E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_justice" to="#suzman">

<from>The MINISTER OF JUSTICE:</from>

<p>The information is not readily available in the Department. In an effort to be of assistance to the hon member, the following information for the period 1 July 1987 to 30 June 1988 was obtained from the Central Statistical Service:</p>

<p>Persons convicted on charges in terms of the Intimidation Act, No 72 of 1982:</p>

<table>

<tr>

<td><p>Whites</p></td>

<td><p>2</p></td>

</tr>

<tr>

<td><p>Coloureds</p></td>

<td><p>4</p></td>

</tr>

<tr>

<td><p>Indians</p></td>

<td><p>1</p></td>

</tr>

<tr>

<td><p>Blacks</p></td>

<td><p>84</p></td>

</tr>

<tr>

<td><p>TOTAL</p></td>

<td><p>91</p></td>

</tr>

</table>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Aliens charged/convicted</heading>

<question by="#van_der_merwe" to="#minister_of_justice">

<num>629.</num>

<from>Mr S S VAN DER MERWE</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Justice:</p>

<p>(a) How many aliens were (i) charged with and (ii) convicted of being in the Republic illegally in 1988 and (b) in terms of what statutory provisions were they (i) charged and (ii) convicted?</p>

<p>B1135E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_justice" to="#van_der_merwe">

<from>The MINISTER OF JUSTICE:</from>

<p>The required information is not readily available in the Department. To obtain the information, all court records pertaining to the crimes concerned will have to be examined, which is not economically feasible.</p>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Persons employing aliens: persons charged</heading>

<question by="#van_der_merwe" to="#minister_of_justice">

<num>630.</num>

<from>Mr S S VAN DER MERWE</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Justice:</p>

<p>Whether any persons were convicted in 1988 of employing aliens who were in the Republic illegally; if so, how many?</p>

<p>B1136E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_justice" to="#van_der_merwe">

<from>The MINISTER OF JUSTICE:</from>

<p>The required information is not readily available in the Department. To obtain the information, all court records pertaining to the crimes concerned will have to be examined, which is not economically feasible.</p>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<heading>Black township: sub-economic/low-cost housing</heading>

<question by="#walsh" to="#minister_of_constitutional_development_and_planning">

<num>633.</num>

<from>Mr J J WALSH</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Constitutional Development and Planning:</p>

<p>Whether any sub-economic/low-cost housing has been built in any Black township in the Greater Cape Town area in the past two years; if so, (a) where, (b) when, (c) by whom, and (d) at what selling price per unit, in each case?</p>

<p>B1139E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_constitutional_development_and_planning" to="#walsh">

<from><span class="col_1429-1430" refersTo="page_0753"/>The MINISTER OF CONSTITUTIONAL DEVELOPMENT AND PLANNING:</from>

<p>This matter vests in the Administrator of the Cape Province and he has furnished the following information:</p>

<p>Yes.</p>

<ol>

<li>(a) Lingelethu West</li>

<li>(b) Past two years</li>

<li>(c) Cape Provincial Govenment (Job Creation houses)</li>

<li>(d) &#x00B1;R7 000 each</li>

</ol>

<ol>

<li>(a) Crossroads</li>

<li>(b) Past two years</li>

<li>(c) &#x2014; Topcor (Manpower Agency)<br/>&#x2014; Cape Provincial Government (Self-help Scheme)</li>

<li>(d) &#x2014; Topcor: the prices ranged between R8 000, R9 500 and R11 000<br/>&#x2014; Cape Provincial Government: R7 000 each</li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<heading>&#x201C;White&#x201D; deleted from welfare organizationconstitutions</heading>

<question by="#langley" to="#minister_of_health_services_and_welfare">

<num>65.</num>

<from>Mr T LANGLEY</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Health Services and Welfare:&#x2020;</p>

<p>Whether welfare organizations that apply to his Department for subsidies from the State are required to delete the word &#x201C;White&#x201D; from their constitutions; if so, why; if not, what is the policy of his Department regarding applications for subsidies by welfare organizations with constitutions in which this word still occurs?</p>

<p>B1143E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_health_services_and_welfare" to="#langley">

<from>The MINISTER OF HEALTH SERVICES AND WELFARE:</from>

<p>No. Welfare organization that applies to the Department of Health and Welfare, Administration: House of Assembly, for a subsidy from the State, is required to delete the word &#x201C;White&#x201D; from its constitution.</p>

<p>The Department is concerned with welfare services in respect of the White population group and, regardless of the wording of the constitution of a welfare organization, will consider subsidisation only of the welfare services rendered by that organization to the White population group.</p>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

</debateSection>

</debateSection>

<debateSection name="#house_of_representatives">

<heading><span class="col_1433-1434" refersTo="page_0755"/>HOUSE OF REPRESENTATIVES</heading>

<debateSection name="#questions">

<heading>QUESTIONS</heading>

<p>&#x2020;Indicates translated version.</p>

<p><i>For written reply:</i></p>

<p><i>General Affairs:</i></p>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Information on SBDC/Hive of Industry/Factorama</heading>

<question by="#dietrich" to="#minister_of_economic_affairs_and_technology">

<num>15.</num>

<from>Mr W J DIETRICH</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Economic Affairs and Technology:</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) Whether he will furnish information on certain activities of the Small Business Development Corporation; if not, why not; if so, on what categories of activities;</li>

<li>(2) (a) what is the leasing rate per square metre at (i) the Hive of Industry in Port Elizabeth and (ii) Factorama in Gelvandale and (b) what was the tenant turn-over at (i) the Hive and (ii) Factorama during each of the latest specified three financial years for which information is available;</li>

<li>(3) (a) how many current tenants at the Hive were (i) unemployed and (ii) employed elsewhere immediately before being accommodated there and (b) what skills do these tenants have in each case;</li>

<li>(4) whether any loans were granted by the said corporation to the informal sector in the northern areas of Port Elizabeth during the past three financial years; if so, in respect of each such financial year, (a) what (i) total amount was granted in such loans and (ii) skills were involved, (b) what collateral was required for these loans and (c) how long did it take, on average, to process each such loan?</li>

</ol>

<p>C105E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_economic_affairs_and_technology" to="#dietrich">

<from>The MINISTER OF ECONOMIC AFFAIRS AND TECHNOLOGY:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1) Yes, in respect of broad policy matters and progress with programmes of the Small Business Development Corporation. Since the SBDC is a public company, its accountability with regard to specific business information and the handling of individual cases is limited to the corporation&#x2019;s Board of Directors. However, individual members of Parliament are at liberty to approach the management of the relevant regional office of the SBDC directly in connection with individual problem cases.</li>

<li>(2), (3) and (4) Fall away in view of the reply to part (1).</li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Representations submitted to Group Areas Board</heading>

<question by="#redcliffe" to="#minister_of_constitutional_development_and_planning">

<num>16.</num>

<from>Mr C R REDCLIFFE</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Constitutional Development and Planning:</p>

<p>Whether any written representations were submitted to the Group Areas Board at its sitting in Uitenhage on 15 March 1989; if so, (a) how many (i) organizations, and (ii) individuals submitted such representations, (b) what are the names of these (i) organizations and (ii) individuals and (c) what was the purport of the representations submitted by each of these (i) organizations and (ii) individuals?</p>

<p>C107E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_constitutional_development_and_planning" to="#redcliffe">

<from>The MINISTER OF CONSTITUTIONAL DEVELOPMENT AND PLANNING:</from>

<p>Yes.</p>

<ol>

<li>(a) 2

<ol>

<li>(i) 2</li>

<li>(ii) None</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(b)

<ol>

<li>(i) The Human Rights Trust; and Legal Resources Centre</li>

<li>(ii) Falls away.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(c)

<ol>

<li>(i) The Human Rights Trust is opposed to the proclamation of the remaining portions of Kabah for White and/or Indian and/or Coloured development.<br/>Negotiations concerning the future of the area should be conducted between the present and the past inhabitants of Kabah on the one hand and the Government or its representatives on the other hand.<br/>The Legal Resources Centre requested that the group areas investigation be called off and the matter <span class="col_1435-1436" refersTo="page_0756"/>be referred to the Free Settlement Board.</li>

<li>(ii) Falls away.</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<p><i>Own Affairs:</i></p>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Facilities provided for residents of specified areas</heading>

<question by="#rabie" to="#minister_of_local_government_and_housing">

<num>17.</num>

<from>Mr J A RABIE</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Local Government and Housing:&#x2020;</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) Whether his Department intends providing the residents of (a) Bethal, (b) Volksrust, (c) Amersfoort, (d) Amsterdam, (e) Wakkerstroom and (f) Ermelo with (i) accommodation, (ii) school buildings and</li>

<li>(iii) community facilities; if not, (aa) why not and (bb) when is it expected that his Department will attend to these matters; if so,</li>

<li>(2) (a) what is the nature of the facilities to be provided, (b) when is it expected that construction will (i) commence and (ii) be completed, and (c) what total amount has been allocated or is it estimated will be allocated for the abovementioned purposes, in each case;</li>

<li>(3) whether he will make a statement on the matter?</li>

</ol>

<p>C51E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_local_government_and_housing" to="#rabie">

<from>The MINISTER OF LOCAL GOVERNMENT AND HOUSING:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1) The Department continues to fulfil its normal role by identifying housing needs in the towns mentioned and to make available the necessary funds to Local Authorities.

<ol>

<li>(a) (i), (ii) and (iii) Bethal Town Council has commenced with the planning of a project for the installation of services and an application for a loan is awaited. As soon as the town develops, housing, schools and community facilities will be provided in accordance with the need.

<ol>

<li>(aa) and (bb) Fall away.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(b) (i), (ii) and (iii) Volksrust Town Council and the Department are in the process of negotiating a project for the installation of services. As soon as the town develops, housing, schools and community facilities will be provided in accordance with the need.

<ol>

<li>(aa) and (bb) Fall away.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(c), (d) and (f) (i), (ii) and (iii) No application for projects have as yet been received from the Town Councils of Amersfoort, Amsterdam and Ermelo, but the Department is in contact with the Councils mentioned and is prepared to lend support with planning and financing in accordance with the need.

<ol>

<li>(aa) and (bb) Fall away.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(e)

<ol>

<li>(i) Wakkerstroom Town Council is preparing an application for the financing of a project for five houses.</li>

<li>(ii), (iii) and (aa) A school and community facilities are not warranted at this stage.

<ol>

<li>(bb) In accordance with the need.</li>

</ol></li>

</ol></li>

</ol></li>

<li>(2) (a), (b), (i), (ii) and (c) Unknown at this stage.</li>

<li>(3) No.</li>

</ol>

</answer>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Additional housing projects for certain areas</heading>

</writtenStatements>

<question by="#rabie" to="#minister_of_local_government_and_housing">

<num>18.</num>

<from>Mr J A RABIE</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Local Government and Housing:&#x2020;</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) Whether his Department envisages any additional housing projects for (a) Standerton, (b) Middelburg, (c) Witbank, (d) Barberton, (e) Sabie, (f) Nelspruit and (g) White River; if not, why not; if so, what are the relevant details;</li>

<li>(2) whether his Department intends supplying electricity to the houses at Sabie; if not, why not; if so, when;</li>

<li>(3) whether the structural defects in buildings at Graskop falling under his Department are to be repaired; if not, why not; if so, when;</li>

<li>(4) whether communithy facilities are envisaged for Graskop and Sabie; if not, why not; if so, (a) when is it expected that construction will (i) commence and (ii) be completed, and (b) what total amount has <span class="col_1437-1438" refersTo="page_0757"/>been allocated for this purpose, in each case?</li>

</ol>

<p>C52E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_local_government_and_housing" to="#rabie">

<from>The MINISTER OF LOCAL GOVERNMENT AND HOUSING:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1) The provision for housing is the responsibility of the Local Authority concerned and my Department makes available loans for this purpose.

<ol>

<li>(a) The Standerton Town Council envisages a self-help project of 15 houses for which funds have already been allocated.</li>

<li>(b) The Middelburg Town Council envisages a self-help project of 145 houses for which funds have already been allocated.</li>

<li>(c) The Witbank Town Council has commenced with the planning of 18 houses.</li>

<li>(d) The Barberton Town Council has commenced with the planning of 25 houses.</li>

<li>(e) The Sabie Town Council has commenced with the planning of 30 houses.</li>

<li>(f) The Nelspruit Town Council has commenced with the erection of 20 self-help houses and a further 20 houses are being planned.</li>

<li>(g) In the case of White River, the Department has initially undertaken the development (White River Extention 13), as the area falls outside the Municipal boundaries of the Local Authority. Township establishment is being awaited.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(2) No.<br/>

The Sabie Town Council&#x2019;s application for financing and provision of services for 150 erven, which includes electricity, has been approved.</li>

<li>(3) The dwellings at Graskop erected by the Town Council, were recently completed. A further 20 houses will be erected shortly.<br/>

Structural defects have been repaired under retention.</li>

<li>(4) As the Sabie Town Council will shortly begin with the upgrading of services, a start to further projects is being kept in abeyance until the work has been completed. A community hall will in all probability be built in the 1990/91 financial year. The development of Graskop has not been finalized. The provision of community facilities is receiving attention.

<ol>

<li>(a), (i), (ii) and (b) Fall away.</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

</answer>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Eldorado Park: complaints about businesspremises</heading>

</writtenStatements>

<question by="#rabie" to="#minister_of_local_government_and_housing">

<num>20.</num>

<from>Mr J A RABIE</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Local Government and Housing:&#x2020;</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) Whether he has received any complaints about the sale by his Department of business premises 4925, 4080, 4063, 4068, 4087, 4079, 4047 and 4069 in Eldorado Park; if so, (a) (i) to whom and (ii) at what prices were these premises sold and (b) (i) what is the purport of the abovementioned complaints and (ii) from whom were they received;</li>

<li>(2) whether an investigation was ordered into these complaints; if not, why not; if so, (a) what was the result of the investigation and (b) what steps were taken by his Department in connection with the complaints;</li>

<li>(3) whether any of the premises concerned were resold on an irregular basis at a later stage; if so, what are the relevant details;</li>

<li>(4) whether he will make a statement on the matter?</li>

</ol>

<p>C55E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_local_government_and_housing" to="#rabie">

<from>The MINISTER OF LOCAL GOVERNMENT AND HOUSING:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1) Yes, in respect of stands 4080, 4063 and 4925 approximately 12 months after the sale thereof.

<ol>

<li>(a) (i) and (ii)</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

<table>

<tr>

<td><p>Stand No.</p></td>

<td><p>Purchaser</p></td>

<td><p>Price</p></td>

</tr>

<tr>

<td><p>4063</p></td>

<td><p>M &#x0026; M Motors</p></td>

<td><p>R372 955</p></td>

</tr>

<tr>

<td><p>4080</p></td>

<td><p>Cyrkurt Construction</p></td>

<td><p>R126 000</p></td>

</tr>

</table>

<p>(Stand No. 4925 Eldorado Park Extension 4, is/was the property of the Johannesburg City Council.)</p>

<ol>

<li><span class="col_1439-1440" refersTo="page_0758"/>(b)

<ol>

<li>(i) The involvement of certain individuals with companies to whom the stands were sold and against whom certain allegations of fraud were made, has been pointed out.</li>

<li>(ii) United Civic Association.</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

<ol>

<li>(2) No, no allegations were made against the Department, Minister or the Development Board in this regard.

<ol>

<li>(a) Falls away.</li>

<li>(b) It was noted that the complete memorandum of the United Civic Association has been referred to the Advocate-General.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(3) Not according to information available.</li>

<li>(4) No.</li>

</ol>

</answer>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Contracts awarded for housing projects</heading>

</writtenStatements>

<question by="#kippen" to="#minister_of_local_government_and_housing">

<num>31.</num>

<from>Mr C J KIPPEN</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Local Government and Housing:</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) Whether his Department awarded any contracts to developers in the private sector, including utility companies, for housing projects falling within the area of jurisdiction of the Durban regional office of his Department; if so, (a) how many tenders were called for in each municipal area in the (i) 1985-86, (ii) 1986-87, (iii) 1987-88 and (iv) 1988-89 financial years, (b) what was the amount tendered by each tenderer in each of these financial years and (c) who were the successful tenderers in each instance;</li>

<li>(2) whether these tenders were advertised in the media; if not, by what means were they advertised;</li>

<li>(3) whether he will furnish particulars of the main differences in respect of the tenders submitted for each of these projects; if not, why not; if so, what are they;</li>

<li>(4) whether any complaints were received from any tenderers; if so, what was the (a) nature and (b) outcome of each such complaint?</li>

</ol>

<ol>

<li>(1) Yes

<ol>

<li>(a)

<ol>

<li>(i) None</li>

<li>(ii) None</li>

<li>(iii) Two contracts at Richards Bay</li>

<li>(iv) None</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(b)

<ol>

<li>(i) Falls away</li>

<li>(ii) Falls away</li>

<li>(iii) Richards Bay:<br/>Erection of 12 dwellings Grinnaker Construction (Pty) Ltd R220 000<br/>Derreg Construction R223 109 Fort Construction R231 500<br/>Alexander Hamilton Construction R238 991<br/>Richards Bay: Erection of 28 dwellings Fort Construction R608 600<br/>Stocks Natal Housing.R679 679 Grinnaker Construction (Pty) Ltd R681 529</li>

<li>(iv) Falls away.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(c) Grinnaker Construction (Pty) Ltd for the erection of 12 dwellings Fort Construction for the erection of 28 dwellings.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(2) No. State Tender Bulletin</li>

<li>(3) Yes. Aforesaid tender prices.</li>

<li>(4) No.

<ol>

<li>(a) Falls away.</li>

<li>(b) Falls away.</li>

</ol></li>

</ol>

<heading>Departmental component: dam construction</heading>

<question by="#redcliffe" to="#minister_of_the_budget_and_agriculture">

<num>43.</num>

<from>Mr C R REDCLIFFE</from>

<p>asked the Minister of the Budget and Agriculture:</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) Whether any component of any Department falling under the Administration: House of Representatives constructed or caused to be constructed any dams in the 1985-86, 1986-87, 1987-88 or 1988-89 financial year; if not, why not; if so, (a) (i) where and (ii) when was each such dam constructed, (b) what was the (i) total cost and (ii) size of each and (c) for what purpose was each constructed;</li>

<li>(2) (a) what is the name of the departmental component responsible for water supply matters and (b) under which Department of the said Administration does it fall?</li>

</ol>

<from>The MINISTER OF THE BUDGET AND AGRICULTURE:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1)

<ol>

<li>(a)

<ol>

<li>(i) Yes, at Rodefontein, Plettenberg Bay.</li>

<li>(ii) Under construction since April 1988.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(b)

<ol>

<li>(i) Cost as at 31 March 1989, R4 031 404,38.</li>

</ol></li>

<ol>

<debateSection name="#house_of_delegates">

<heading><span class="col_1443-1444" refersTo="page_0760"/>HOUSE OF DELEGATES</heading>

<debateSection name="#questions">

<heading>QUESTIONS</heading>

<p>&#x2020;Indicates translated version.</p>

<p><i>For written reply:</i></p>

<p><i>General Affairs:</i></p>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Religious institutions: foreign students</heading>

<question by="#seedat" to="#minister_of_home_affairs">

<num>16.</num>

<from>Mr Y I SEEDAT</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Home Affairs:</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) What is the policy of his Department in regard to the admission to South Africa of foreign students wishing to enrol at recognized religious institutions in South Africa when (a) such education is not available in the prospective students&#x2019; countries of origin and (b) the institutions concerned receive no assistance from the South African Government;</li>

<li>(2) whether, during the latest specified period of 12 months for which statistics are available, his Department granted any permits to foreign students to study at recognized religious institutions in South Africa; if not, why not; if so, (a) what are the names of the institutions involved and (b) how many foreign students were granted such permits in respect of each of these institutions?</li>

</ol>

<p>D95E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_home_affairs" to="#seedat">

<from>The MINISTER OF HOME AFFAIRS:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1) (a) and (b) As was undertaken during the discussion of my Budget Vote this year, I have ordered an investigation into the matter in question. The hon member will be advised of my decision on this matter as soon as possible.</li>

<li>(2) Separate statistics in respect of foreign students studying at religious institutions are not kept.</li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>

<writtenStatements>

<heading>Industries in Ezakheni: benefits</heading>

<question by="#jumuna" to="#minister_of_constitutional_development_and_planning">

<num>20.</num>

<from>Mr N JUMUNA</from>

<p>asked the Minister of Constitutional Development and Planning:</p>

<ol>

<li>(1) Whether any industries in Ezakheni enjoy benefits under the decentralization scheme; if so, (a) how many and (b) what benefits;</li>

<li>(2) how many (a) Indian, (b) Coloured, (c) Black and (d) White persons currently employed in these industries were (i) local inhabitants living within the Klip River district at the time of their appointment and (ii) from outside the Klip River district at the time of their appointment but are now residing within this district;</li>

<li>(3) in respect of what date is this information furnished?</li>

</ol>

<p>D103E</p>

</question>

<answer by="#minister_of_constitutional_development_and_planning" to="#jumuna">

<from>The MINISTER OF CONSTITUTIONAL DEVELOPMENT AND PLANNING:</from>

<ol>

<li>(1) Yes.

<ol>

<li>(a) 60</li>

<li>(b) Short- and/or long-term concessions under the standard package of incentives.</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(2) Estimated at:

<ol>

<li>(i)

<ol>

<li>(a) 446 Indians</li>

<li>(b) 61 Coloureds</li>

<li>(c) 9 756 Blacks; and</li>

<li>(d) 420 Whites;</li>

</ol></li>

<li>(ii)

<ol>

<li>(a) a small number;</li>

<li>(b) a small number;</li>

<li>(c) a small number;</li>

<li>(d) 119;</li>

</ol></li>

</ol></li>

<li>(3) For the period 1 April 1982 to 31 March 1989.</li>

</ol>

</answer>

</writtenStatements>
